📚 108 Curriculum Special Needs Area: Social Skills 🎭 Differentiated division of labor line drill (Yu🦁, Jun🐻, He🐼, Jia🦊） 🧭 CBT behavioral causal decision simulator (Choice & Consequence) 📄 One page per class A4 · Dedicated to classroom projection group drills
🌟 Classroom Theater: Four representative characters of special education children (Persona) practice division of labor and scaffolding guidance
🦁 Yu (ASD)
Protagonist challenger · Overcome speaking anxiety
Arrange the first line of dialogue, with direct gaze between the eyebrows and clear instructions with body movements.
🐻 Xiaobian (EBD)
Collaborative Interactors · Practice patient listening
Restrain impatience and rush to talk, and practice gentle and decent responses and sportsmanship that can afford to lose.
🐼 He (ASD/LD)
Warm supporters · Build successful experiences
Give recognition and support to peers, and speak openly in safety groups.
🦊 Jia (ASD)
Order leader · Positive class leader
Host the announcement of rules, lead the applause and applause, and turn the persistence of rules into organizational power.

Week 1 - [Welcome Back to School] Say Hello Loud - Getting to Know New Classmates and Building a Sense of Security

📅 September 1, 2026 (Tuesday) · 🌟 Energetic Greeting Officer
🎭 Role-playing Theater: 4 Special Education Children's Practical Script Yu🦁, Jun🐻, He🐼, Jia🦊 : Differentiated division of labor in dialogue rehearsal
🎬 Today's drill scenario:On the first day of school, everyone walks into the classroom, faces a new teacher and new classmates, and practices making eye contact, smiling, and clearly introducing themselves!
🦁 Yu took the initiative to greet her Yu
[Action Guide]Stand tall with your head held high and chest out, look straight ahead, smile with the corners of your mouth turned up, and wave your right hand to greet others.
Good morning everyone! I'm Yu, nice to meet you all in the new semester!
💡 Scaffolding guidance: Guide Yu to overcome anxiety in unfamiliar situations and practice proactive verbal greetings.
🐻 Jun introduces himself generously Jun Tongxue
[Action Guide]Let your arms hang naturally at your sides, take a step forward with steady steps, and speak loudly.
"I'm Jun! I love sports and building blocks, and I'm so happy to be in the same class as everyone!"
💡 Scaffolding guidance: guides Jun to transform excess energy into confident and generous self-expression.
🐼 A gentle response from He With classmates
[Action Guide]Gently place your hands in front of your stomach, bow deeply to everyone, and look at them with gentle eyes.
"Hello everyone, I'm He. I often help you tidy up the classroom!"
💡 Scaffolding guidance: Encourage speaking up in safe situations to boost self-efficacy.
🦊 A warm welcome to Jia Jia
[Action Guide]Hold up the class welcome sign and lead the applause three times to create a lively welcoming atmosphere.
"Welcome everyone to the classroom! The four of us will definitely be the best team!"
💡 Scaffolding guidance: Encourage Jia to leverage his affability and leadership strengths.
🎮 Amusement Park Practice: Causal Simulation of Behavioral Choices and Forking Paths CBT (Cognitive Behavioral Therapy) & PBS (Positive Behavioral Support)
🧭 Behavioral Choice Test:In the scenario test of "greeting each other happily on the first day of school versus greeting loudly", if you were in this situation, which behavioral path would you choose?
💥 💥 Impulsive temper tantrums / Refusal to cooperate
[Behavioral Procedure]Shouting loudly, pushing people, or sulking and running away.
❌ Consequences of the behavior: The classmate was frightened to tears, the activity was forced to stop, everyone was in a bad mood, and I lost a star... I feel so regretful!
⚠️ Your impulsive brain is on fire! Next time, try stepping on the brakes and taking three deep breaths!
🌟 🌟 Superpower:👋 Smiling and Greeting Superman
[Behavioral Procedure]Take a deep breath and apply the brakes, using the "👋 Smiling Greeting Superman" to express yourself and act politely.
🎉 A resounding success! Your peers witnessed your maturity and respect, and the whole class applauded and cheered for you. Every student received a gold star of honor!
🏆 Fantastic! The rational mind triumphs, successfully mastering social superpowers!
🌟 Results of the theater practice challenge: ⭐ ⭐ ⭐
💮 Teacher's signature: [ ]

Week 2: Eye Contact and a Warm Smile – The Magic of Nonverbal Communication

📅 September 8, 2026 (Tuesday) · ✨ Focused Gaze
🎭 Role-playing Theater: 4 Special Education Children's Practical Script Yu🦁, Jun🐻, He🐼, Jia🦊 : Differentiated division of labor in dialogue rehearsal
🎬 Today's drill scenario:If the other person looks around while you are speaking, it will make you feel disrespected. The four partners practice "looking at each other between the eyebrows + smiling for three seconds"!
🦁 Yu focusing on the space between the eyebrows Yu
[Action Guide]Focus your eyes on the space between the other person's eyebrows, lean forward slightly, and nod earnestly.
"Little Jun, look! I'm looking at your forehead right now, I'm listening attentively to what you're saying!"
💡 Scaffolding guidance: Guide Yu to use the visual focus between his eyebrows instead of looking directly at his eyes to reduce social anxiety.
🐻 A young man who feels respected Jun Tongxue
[Action Guide]Put down the toy ball in your hand, face Yu, place your hands flat, and smile in response.
"Yu, it feels so good to hear you talking to me. I know you're listening!"
💡 Scaffolding guidance: guide participants to experience positive feedback of being respected and listened to.
🐼 Little He with a gentle gaze With classmates
[Action Guide]Slightly raise the corners of your mouth, look warmly at your peers, and gently clap your hands.
"Everyone was smiling at each other while we were talking; the atmosphere was so warm!"
💡 Scaffolding guidance: guides and reinforces the consistency between eye contact and facial expressions.
🦊 Watching the timekeeper, Jia Jia
[Action Guide]Extend three fingers to show a "three-second countdown" and confirm that both parties are looking at each other.
"Stare intently for three seconds, then smile! The eye contact is a resounding success; everyone passes!"
💡 Scaffolding Guidelines: Guiding Jia to master quantifiable standards for nonverbal communication.
🎮 Amusement Park Practice: Causal Simulation of Behavioral Choices and Forking Paths CBT (Cognitive Behavioral Therapy) & PBS (Positive Behavioral Support)
🧭 Behavioral Choice Test:In the situational test of "eye contact and warm smile", if you were in this situation, which behavioral path would you choose?
💥 💥 Impulsive temper tantrums / Refusal to cooperate
[Behavioral Procedure]Shouting loudly, pushing people, or sulking and running away.
❌ Consequences of the behavior: The classmate was frightened to tears, the activity was forced to stop, everyone was in a bad mood, and I lost a star... I feel so regretful!
⚠️ Your impulsive brain is on fire! Next time, try stepping on the brakes and taking three deep breaths!
🌟 🌟 Superpower:👁️ Eyes and forehead spotlight
[Behavioral Procedure]Take a deep breath and apply the brakes, using the "👁️ Eyes, eyebrows, spotlight" express and act politely
🎉 A resounding success! Your peers witnessed your maturity and respect, and the whole class applauded and cheered for you. Every student received a gold star of honor!
🏆 Fantastic! The rational mind triumphs, successfully mastering social superpowers!
🌟 Results of the theater practice challenge: ⭐ ⭐ ⭐

Week 3 - Five-Level Volume Dashboard - Adjusting Speaking Volume in Different Situations

📅 September 15, 2026 (Tuesday) · 🎙️ The Volume Magician
🎭 Role-playing Theater: 4 Special Education Children's Practical Script Yu🦁, Jun🐻, He🐼, Jia🦊 : Differentiated division of labor in dialogue rehearsal
🎬 Today's drill scenario:In the library and group discussion, everyone practiced turning their throat knob to the correct volume level!
🦁 Yu adjusting the volume Yu
[Action Guide]Place your index finger in front of your lips to indicate quiet, and lower your throat to produce a steady and gentle voice.
"It's library time now, I'm switching my voice to a whisper!"
💡 Scaffolding guidance: Guide Yu to switch volume levels autonomously in different situations.
🐻 Little Jun, who pressed the brakes on his loud voice Jun Tongxue
[Action Guide]Place your right hand on your throat and feel the vibration. Intentionally soften your tone and speak softly.
"Phew~ I've turned the loudspeaker down! This is a group discussion, so I'll use a volume control of two!"
💡 Scaffolding guidance: guides students to perceive the intensity of throat vibrations and self-regulate their speaking volume.
🐼 The soft-spoken He With classmates
[Action Guide]Take a small step closer to your peer, and speak in a clear but gentle voice, like a breeze.
"Jun, your voice has become gentler; it's just the right size for us to hear each other during our discussion!"
💡 Scaffolding guidance: Guide and experience the comfortable feeling of communication that comes with appropriate volume.
🦊 Volume Tester - Jia Jia
[Action Guide]Hold the volume indicator card, point to the green 2-minute mark, and nod vigorously in affirmation.
"The volume is precisely locked at two-thirds! It's not noisy, yet clearly audible; the classroom is perfectly quiet!"
💡 Scaffolding Guidelines: Guide Jia to strengthen the monitoring and feedback of the collective sound environment.
🎮 Amusement Park Practice: Causal Simulation of Behavioral Choices and Forking Paths CBT (Cognitive Behavioral Therapy) & PBS (Positive Behavioral Support)
🧭 Behavioral Choice Test:In the scenario test of the "Voice Volume Five-Level Dashboard", if you were in this situation, which behavioral path would you choose?
💥 💥 Impulsive temper tantrums / Refusal to cooperate
[Behavioral Procedure]Shouting loudly, pushing people, or sulking and running away.
❌ Consequences of the behavior: The classmate was frightened to tears, the activity was forced to stop, everyone was in a bad mood, and I lost a star... I feel so regretful!
⚠️ Your impulsive brain is on fire! Next time, try stepping on the brakes and taking three deep breaths!
🌟 🌟 Superpower:🎛️ Magical volume control on the dashboard
[Behavioral Procedure]Take a deep breath and apply the brakes, using the "🎛️ volume dashboard" to adjust your tone politely in your speech and actions.
🎉 A resounding success! Your peers witnessed your maturity and respect, and the whole class applauded and cheered for you. Every student received a gold star of honor!
🏆 Fantastic! The rational mind triumphs, successfully mastering social superpowers!

Week 4 - [Teachers' Day] Seeing the hard work of teachers, let's sincerely say "Thank you."

📅 September 22, 2026 (Tuesday) · 🍎 Little Role Models of Respecting Teachers
🎭 Role-playing Theater: 4 Special Education Children's Practical Script Yu🦁, Jun🐻, He🐼, Jia🦊 : Differentiated division of labor in dialogue rehearsal
🎬 Today's drill scenario:It's Teacher's Day, and four little heroes practice giving sincere thanks and gifts to their mentors and special education teachers who have worked hard to accompany them!
🦁 Yu bowing respectfully Yu
[Action Guide]Place both hands on either side of your thighs, bend your waist at a 45-degree angle, and bow deeply to the teacher.
"Hello teacher! Thank you for patiently teaching me deep breathing every day. Happy Teacher's Day!"
💡 Scaffolding Guidelines: Guide Yu to implement the complete teacher respect etiquette of "standing at attention, bowing, and gazing".
🐻 Little Jun, loudly proclaiming your love Jun Tongxue
[Action Guide]Respectfully present the hand-drawn gold heart card with both hands, and speak with sincere and bright eyes.
"Thank you for your hard work, teacher! I cut this card myself, and I'll definitely pay more attention in class from now on!"
💡 Scaffolding guidance: guides students to transform impulses into respect for teachers and self-commitment.
🐼 Here's some warm water for you, He. With classmates
[Action Guide]Hold the cup of warm water with both hands and gently place it on the table, with a warm smile on your face.
"Teacher, you seem thirsty from talking. Please have a sip of warm water. Thank you for taking care of us every day!"
💡 Scaffolding guidance: guiding and practicing considerate actions through observation of daily life.
🦊 Respect for Teachers Commander - Jia Jia
[Action Guide]Raise the hand gesture and lead all students in saying thank you and applauding enthusiastically.
"Salute! Thank you, teacher. Wishing you happiness every day and a Happy Teachers' Day!"
💡 Scaffolding guidance: Guiding Jia to foster a positive atmosphere of gratitude within the group.
🎮 Amusement Park Practice: Causal Simulation of Behavioral Choices and Forking Paths CBT (Cognitive Behavioral Therapy) & PBS (Positive Behavioral Support)
🧭 Behavioral Choice Test:In the scenario test of "【Teacher's Day Special 9/28】", if you were in this situation, which behavioral path would you choose?
💥 💥 Impulsive temper tantrums / Refusal to cooperate
[Behavioral Procedure]Shouting loudly, pushing people, or sulking and running away.
❌ Consequences of the behavior: The classmate was frightened to tears, the activity was forced to stop, everyone was in a bad mood, and I lost a star... I feel so regretful!
⚠️ Your impulsive brain is on fire! Next time, try stepping on the brakes and taking three deep breaths!
🌟 🌟 Superpower:💌 Little Ambassador of Respect and Gratitude to Teachers
[Behavioral Procedure]Take a deep breath and apply the brakes, using the "💌 Little Ambassador of Respect for Teachers and Gratitude" to express and act politely.
🎉 A resounding success! Your peers witnessed your maturity and respect, and the whole class applauded and cheered for you. Every student received a gold star of honor!
🏆 Fantastic! The rational mind triumphs, successfully mastering social superpowers!

Week 5 - Mid-Autumn Festival Mooncake and Pomelo Sharing Party: Food Sharing and Courtesy

📅 September 29, 2026 (Tuesday) · 🥮 Sharing Little Fairy
🎭 Role-playing Theater: 4 Special Education Children's Practical Script Yu🦁, Jun🐻, He🐼, Jia🦊 : Differentiated division of labor in dialogue rehearsal
🎬 Today's drill scenario:At the Mid-Autumn Festival celebration, everyone lined up to get snacks. When faced with the most popular egg yolk pastry, everyone practiced taking one and offering it to their friends!

📅 October 13, 2026 (Tuesday) · 🌡️ Body Detective

"My castle has been knocked down! My heart is pounding, the thermometer reads four! I'm so angry, but let me take a deep breath first!"
💡 Scaffolding guidance: Guide Yu to identify warning signs in the body such as a rapid heartbeat and flushed face, and state the level instead of physical outbursts.

"I'm sorry! I was running too fast and knocked over your blocks! Let me help you pick them up and rebuild!"

Week 8 - The Amazing Turtle Braking Technique - When angry, retract your shell, take three deep breaths, and count to ten.

📅 October 20, 2026 (Tuesday) · 🐢 Braking Master
🎭 Role-playing Theater: 4 Special Education Children's Practical Script Yu🦁, Jun🐻, He🐼, Jia🦊 : Differentiated division of labor in dialogue rehearsal
🎬 Today's drill scenario:When a toy is snatched or accidentally bumped into, everyone practice immediately making a homemade braking motion by "hugging your chest and counting to ten"!
🦁 Yu, who shrank his car and braked. Yu
[Action Guide]Cross your arms in front of your chest, tuck your chin to your collarbone, close your eyes, and slowly and evenly inhale and exhale three times.
"Retract into your turtle shell, take a deep breath, one, two, three! I'll put my fist back in, and I'll speak properly in ten seconds!"
💡 Scaffolding guidance: Guide Yu to block aggressive impulses through proprioceptive stimulation of crossing his chest.
🐻 The little farmer counting to ten Jun Tongxue
[Action Guide]Place your fists on your abdomen, stand firmly on the ground, count down to one in your mind, and exhale a long breath.
"Ten, nine, eight... three, two, one! Whew~ The anger has subsided, I've successfully hit the brakes!"
💡 Scaffolding guidance: The guide uses a countdown timer to switch the brain's hot zone to the prefrontal cortex cooling zone.
🐼 Gentle and protective little one With classmates
[Action Guide]Stand quietly by your side, without rushing or mocking, and gently count the beats to help your partner relax their shoulders.
"Good job, Jun! Slowly relax your shoulders. The turtle shell protects you. You're amazing for not hitting anyone!"
💡 Scaffolding guidance: Guiding and providing a safe space for peers to calm down.
🦊 Award presenter for brake compliance - Jia Jia
[Action Guide]Hold up the green turtle badge sticker and stick it on the chest of the partner who successfully stepped on the brake.
"Control your fists and use your brain; the turtle's braking technique is the best in the world! We are all little heroes of emotional self-discipline!"
💡 Scaffolding guidance: guiding and strengthening self-discipline, honor, and identity.
🎮 Amusement Park Practice: Causal Simulation of Behavioral Choices and Forking Paths CBT (Cognitive Behavioral Therapy) & PBS (Positive Behavioral Support)

Week 21 - [Final Assessment] Treat red crosses as a brain upgrade, and don't tear up the exam paper.

📅 January 19, 2027 (Tuesday) · 🆙 Smart Upgrade Officer
🎭 Role-playing Theater: 4 Special Education Children's Practical Script Yu🦁, Jun🐻, He🐼, Jia🦊 : Differentiated division of labor in dialogue rehearsal
🎬 Today's drill scenario:When you receive a test paper full of red crosses and feel like crumpling it up and crying, practice smoothing out the test paper, taking out a red pen, looking up the answers in the dictionary, and correcting the questions!
🦁 Yu who wrote the wrong answer on the exam paper Yu
[Action Guide]Take three deep breaths, place the exam paper flat on the table, do not crumple the paper or cry, and take out a red pen and a dictionary.
"It's okay to make mistakes on the exam! The red crosses are reminders that my brain needs an upgrade. I'll look it up in the dictionary and correct it now!"
💡 Scaffolding guidance: guides you to transform setbacks into a growth mindset, and corrections become an opportunity to upgrade your brain.
🐻 Little Jun almost tore up the exam paper Jun Tongxue
[Action Guide]Seeing Yu diligently correcting his work, gently flatten the ball-shaped test paper and take out the eraser.
"Phew~ I almost got angry and tore the paper up! But the teacher said mistakes are the best teachers, so I'll rewrite it!"
💡 Scaffolding guidance: guide students to control impulsive destructive behavior and learn to face setbacks.
🐼 Corrections to the Mini Dictionary - He With classmates
[Action Guide]Actively turn to the page of the dictionary and point to the correct character to compare its radical with Yu's.
"Yu, look, the right side of this character is the 'strength' of 'power.' Let's write it together once and you'll remember it forever!"
💡 Scaffolding guidance: Guiding and providing peer-specific academic scaffolding support.
🦊 Brain Upgrade Witness - Jia Jia
[Action Guide]Stamp a "Brain Upgrade 2.0" gold seal on Yu's corrected test paper.
"Finding and correcting mistakes yourself is more impressive than writing it correctly from the start! The entire team's brains have been successfully upgraded!"
💡 Scaffolding guidance: Guiding Jia to strengthen self-affirmation and growth mindset.

Week 26: My Personal Body Airbag – Understanding Body Boundaries and the Safe Distance of an Arm

📅 February 23, 2027 (Tuesday) · 🎈 Boundary Guardian
🎭 Role-playing Theater: 4 Special Education Children's Practical Script Yu🦁, Jun🐻, He🐼, Jia🦊 : Differentiated division of labor in dialogue rehearsal
🎬 Today's drill scenario:When queuing or chatting, someone gets too close and makes you uncomfortable. Everyone practice extending one hand to show the distance of an arm's length and gently remind them!
🦁 Yu, the guardian of airbags Yu
[Action Guide]Extend your right arm forward and raise it horizontally, palm facing forward, to make a pause gesture, and look at the other person politely but firmly.
"Jun, please step back! Let's keep an arm's length distance; that's more comfortable for me!"
💡 Scaffolding guidance: Guide Yu to use arm extension movements to clearly establish his own body boundaries.
🐻 Little Jun who took a step back Jun Tongxue
[Action Guide]Upon seeing the pause gesture, immediately take a small step back with both feet, place your hands on your sides, and give an understanding smile.
"Sorry! I was too close! I'll take a step back so we can both comfortably use the airbags!"
💡 Scaffolding guidance: guides students to learn to recognize other people's signals of vacant space and step back immediately.
🐼 Distance Measurement Helper - He With classmates
[Action Guide]Make a hula hoop shape with your hands, stand beside two classmates, and make sure that you do not bump into each other.
"Each person has an invisible airbag; with each person spaced one step apart, everyone can breathe easily and safely!"
💡 Scaffolding guidance: guiding and specifically strengthening the perception of interpersonal spatial boundaries.
🦊 Boundary Safety Inspector - Jia Jia
[Action Guide]Hold the airbag badge and check the social distancing during class interaction.
"Maintaining an arm's length distance, politeness and comfort come first! We are all little gentlemen who know how to respect boundaries!"
💡 Scaffolding Guidelines: Guiding Jia to summarize the social virtue value of interpersonal distance.

[Behavioral Procedure]Shouting loudly, pushing people, or sulking and running away.
❌ Consequences of the behavior: The classmate was frightened to tears, the activity was forced to stop, everyone was in a bad mood, and I lost a star... I feel so regretful!
⚠️ Your impulsive brain is on fire! Next time, try stepping on the brakes and taking three deep breaths!

Week 27: Courageously and Firmly Saying "No" – Bodily Autonomy and Rejecting Violation

📅 March 2, 2027 (Tuesday) · 🛡️ Body Defense Officer
🎭 Role-playing Theater: 4 Special Education Children's Practical Script Yu🦁, Jun🐻, He🐼, Jia🦊 : Differentiated division of labor in dialogue rehearsal
🎬 Today's drill scenario:In the hallway or playground, when classmates push you excessively, pinch your face, or force you to touch your belongings, practice "standing tall, reaching out your hand, making eye contact, and firmly refusing"!
🦁 Yu firmly refused Yu
[Action Guide]Stand firmly on your feet like a tree, extend your right hand with your five fingers facing forward, and look firmly at the other person's brow.
"Please stop! I don't like having my cheeks pinched. Please step back and respect me!"
💡 Scaffolding guidance: guides Yu to overcome people-pleasing and timid reactions, and to clearly declare his self-sovereignty through physical boundaries.
🐻 Little Jun Learns to Respect Jun Tongxue
[Action Guide]Immediately retract your outstretched hand behind your back, take a step back with both feet, and look serious and focused.
"I'm sorry, Yu! I just wanted to play with you. I didn't know you didn't like it. I won't do it again!"
💡 Scaffolding guidance: Guide students to practice immediately withdrawing when they receive a clear rejection signal and learn to empathize with boundaries.
🐼 Guardian of Body Boundaries - He With classmates
[Action Guide]Stand beside your peers to offer support, clasp your hands together in front of your chest, and maintain a calm and composed expression.
"Everyone has their own physical boundaries, and saying no means no. Everyone must respect each other's boundaries!"
💡 Scaffolding guidance: guide and build a peer alliance to support the right to refuse behavior.
🦊 Body Autonomy Oath Officer - Jia Jia
[Action Guide]Hold up the red defense shield symbol and lead all partners to loudly repeat the three-word mantra for protecting the body.
"Say no loudly, leave quickly, and find an adult for help! We have the right to make our own decisions about our bodies!"
💡 Scaffolding Guidelines: Guide students to review and reinforce standard protection procedures for preventing harm.
🎮 Amusement Park Practice: Causal Simulation of Behavioral Choices and Forking Paths CBT (Cognitive Behavioral Therapy) & PBS (Positive Behavioral Support)

💥 💥 Impulsive temper tantrums / Refusal to cooperate

❌ Consequences of the behavior: The classmate was frightened to tears, the activity was forced to stop, everyone was in a bad mood, and I lost a star... I feel so regretful!

💡 Scaffolding guidance: guide students to establish an impulse inhibition chain of "swallowing → wiping mouth → speaking".

Week 37: Campus Safety Without Blind Spots – No Running in Corridors, Keep to the Right on Stairs, Stop, Look, and Listen at Corners

[Action Guide]Let your arms hang naturally at your sides and walk steadily to the right of the white safety line, keeping your eyes on the road ahead.
"Walk slowly in the corridors, every step is safe! No pushing, no running, everyone's responsibility for campus safety!"

🐻 Little Jun who stops to look and listen at the corner Jun Tongxue
[Action Guide]Stop immediately in front of the corner pillar, turn your body slightly to the side, look to the left and then to the right, and only walk across after confirming that no one is there.
"I stopped at the corner, looked left and right to make sure no one was coming! Luckily I stepped on the brakes, otherwise I would have bumped into my classmate!"
💡 Scaffolding guidance: guides the transformation of the sprint reflex into a restrained response such as stopping at corners.
🐼 The little one on the right With classmates
[Action Guide]Lightly hold the metal handrail of the stairs with your right hand, and step up and down the stairs one step at a time, keeping to the right, without stepping into empty spaces.

Week 38: What to do if you step on a landmine? A four-step approach to a sincere apology – defusing interpersonal storms.

📅 May 18, 2027 (Tuesday) · 🤝 Sincere Apology Officer
🎭 Role-playing Theater: 4 Special Education Children's Practical Script Yu🦁, Jun🐻, He🐼, Jia🦊 : Differentiated division of labor in dialogue rehearsal
🎬 Today's drill scenario:In a sudden conflict where a classmate's belongings are accidentally damaged or soiled, the four little heroes practice the standard "four-step sincere apology + concrete repair actions"!
🦁 Little Jun demonstrates the four steps to apologizing. Yu
[Action Guide]Look into the eyes of the victim, place your hands folded in front of your abdomen, bow slightly, and speak sincerely without excuses.
"Yu, I'm so sorry! It was my carelessness that I spilled your watercolors. I'll definitely be more careful next time. Please forgive me!"
💡 Scaffolding guidance: Guiding individuals to let go of their pride and defensiveness, and experience the personal strength that comes from sincerely taking responsibility.
🐻 Yu, having calmed down and accepted the situation. Jun Tongxue
[Action Guide]Take three deep breaths to expel the pent-up air from your chest, look into the other person's eyes, and relax your shoulders.
"Although I was heartbroken just now, seeing how sincerely you apologized, I know you didn't mean it, and I forgive you!"
💡 Scaffolding guidance: Guide Yu to let go of his emotions and release his goodwill of forgiveness after receiving a full apology.
🐼 Small items assisting with cleaning and repair With classmates
[Action Guide]Get wet wipes and a dry cloth, squat down next to the two people and help absorb the stains on the shoes.
"We wiped them together with wet wipes. The red ink hadn't even dried yet, but after wiping them, they looked like cool white sneakers again!"
💡 Scaffolding guidance: Guiding and engaging in collaborative support for substantive relationship repair.
🦊 Relationship Repair Mediator - Jia Jia
[Action Guide]Raise the handshake and reconciliation card to announce that the two partners have successfully resolved their conflict and reconciled.

💡 Scaffolding guidance: Guide Jia to summarize the key value of interpersonal repair in peer interactions.

💡 Scaffolding Guidelines: Guiding Yu to implement basic social dialogue and monetary transaction processes for independent shopping.

[Action Guide]Keep both hands firmly in your pockets and wait until you have paid and received the invoice before opening the packaging bag.

Lesson 53: Inspiration Topic

💡 Inspiration Theme Library · 🔕 Little Heroes of Air Raid Shelter

🦁 Calm and self-disciplined Yu Yu
[Action Guide]Quickly take out the noise-canceling earmuffs from the side pocket of your backpack and put them on. Shrink your body under the table and hug the back of your neck with both hands.
"Phew~ With the mute earmuffs on, the alarm's quieter! I'm safe under the table!"
💡 Scaffolding guidance: guides Yu to overcome sensory overload from sudden, sharp, loud noises and establish a sense of self-protection and security.
🐻 A courageous and responsible young man Jun Tongxue
[Action Guide]Resist the urge to run around, kneel down with both knees on the ground, put your hands behind your head, and keep your mouth tightly closed without playing around.
"Everyone, quickly crouch down! This is evacuation time. We need to remain quiet and protect our heads!"

🐼 Gentle and considerate little He With classmates
[Action Guide]Gently hide behind the table, look at Yu with concern in your eyes, and pat Yu's shoulder to comfort him.
"Yu, don't be afraid, we're all together. The drill will be over in three minutes!"
💡 Scaffolding guidance: guide and leverage the qualities of gentle companionship to build a sense of peer support.
🦊 Enthusiastic and rule-abiding Jia Jia

"Reporting, teacher! Our entire group is in position with our heads covered, and no one is talking or pushing!"

Lesson 54: Inspiration Topic

💡 Inspiration Theme Library · 👕 Tactile Adjustment Expert
🎭 Role-playing Theater: 4 Special Education Children's Practical Script Yu🦁, Jun🐻, He🐼, Jia🦊 : Differentiated division of labor in dialogue rehearsal
🎬 Today's drill scenario:In art class, the clay sticks to their hands, and the tags on their new clothes are itchy. The four little heroes practice expressing their feelings and seeking help!
🦁 Calm and self-disciplined Yu Yu
[Action Guide]When you touch the sticky slime and feel your palms go numb, immediately place both hands flat on the table and take a deep breath, without throwing the slime on the ground.
"Teacher, it feels weird when the clay sticks to my palms. Can I wear gloves before I start shaping it?"
💡 Scaffolding guidance: Guide Yu to actively express tactile defensive sensations and use tools to gradually desensitize him.
🐻 A courageous and responsible young man Jun Tongxue
[Action Guide]Your neck is itchy from the new clothes. Resist the urge to tear the collar and raise your hand to walk towards the teacher.
"Ugh, I'm about to get angry! Because the tag on the back of my shirt is really scratchy, please cut it off for me!"
💡 Scaffolding guidance: Guide Jun to reveal the specific physical reasons before the volcano of anger erupts.
🐼 Gentle and considerate little He With classmates
[Action Guide]Put on thin, transparent gloves, happily pick up the yellow clay and knead it into a small ball, with a bright smile.
"With gloves on, the clay won't stick to my hands! I can build a clay castle too!"
💡 Scaffolding guidance: The sense of successful engagement facilitated by guidance and experience tools.
🦊 Enthusiastic and rule-abiding Jia Jia
[Action Guide]Take the cut-off tag and put it in the trash can, straighten your collar, sit up straight and return to your seat.
"Now that the labels are all removed and I feel better, I can focus on doing art and crafts with everyone!"

💡 Scaffolding guidance: Guiding Jia to repay the kindness of her teachers with heartfelt gratitude, demonstrating the noble brilliance of special education's great love.
🎮 Amusement Park Practice: Causal Simulation of Behavioral Choices and Forking Paths CBT (Cognitive Behavioral Therapy) & PBS (Positive Behavioral Support)
🧭 Behavioral Choice Test:In the scenario of "🌟 Ultimate Special Education Superpower Summary - 49 Inspirations Converging into Light: I am the Captain of My Own Life!", if it were you, which behavioral path would you choose?
💥 💥 Panic Instinct / Impulsive Resistance
[Behavioral Procedure]Bewildered, screaming and running away, refusing to cooperate, or throwing tantrums.
❌ Consequences of the behavior: It leads to danger and panic, frightens peers, and makes things more chaotic instead of being resolved!
⚠️ Your brain is sounding the alarm! Remember to first apply the brakes, take three deep breaths, and activate your social superpowers!
🌟 🌟 Superpower:🌟 Captain of the Boat, Taking Control of Life
[Behavioral Procedure]Calmly activate the "🌟 "Little Captain at the helm of life", follow the steps "When encountering a violent storm (frustration and anger), immediately grasp the steering wheel firmly in the brain and take a deep breath ➔ Use the 49 superpower tools you've learned to find the best solution. ➔ Hold your head high, believe in yourself, and be a kind, brave, and never-say-die helmsman of life."Deal with it calmly
🎉 A resounding success! Successfully protected themselves and others, demonstrating mature and calm superhuman qualities. Everyone received a gold star of honor!
🏆 Fantastic! The rational prefrontal cortex triumphed and passed the test!
